### 2026-11-18 — Player Reborn Playoffs v1, P4: title evidence for the Jersey Exchange, the offseason, multi-year validation — code-puppy-7319ef
- **Wiring:** nothing new to wire: the Finals-clinching game's report already carries `isChampionship` (P2), and the existing exchange rule (`jerseyEligibility`: CHAMPIONSHIP bonus, respect floor 40, real participants, settled game) consumes it. Offers still come only from men in the run's own ledger (rivals who played, or the man who guarded him), nothing is invented.
- **Measured (real GameSim, 40 fixed seeds, 1997, club first, trust 80):** 4 titles, the player on court in the title game in all four (9, 3, 3, 2 min), **0 championship offers**; 22 of 40 runs had an ordinary offer in the playoffs. A bench player rarely guards anyone long enough to be in the ledger, so a title keepsake is rare by design. I did NOT loosen the rule (that would mean inventing relationships); say so if you want it wider (e.g. any Finals opponent he shared the floor with for N minutes).
- **Proof the chain works** (`tests/rebornTitleExchange.test.js`, 5): the real title-game report is settled / a title / a win / participants all on the Finals opponent's real roster; for a man he has met three times (respect 54: enough to be asked, not on its own) the SAME real game offers a keepsake with reasons `CHAMPIONSHIP`, game id, year, result, and nothing when only `isChampionship` is flipped; accepting keeps provenance and is idempotent; not on court / the man did not play / no game id = no offer; a real title won with 0 minutes (seed `honors-real-7`) offers nothing; the offseason (contract judged on the 82 games, rollover, new season at game 1, honour + jersey + history kept, open offers lapse).
- **Multi-year real pipeline:** `scripts/qa/reborn-playoffs-seasons.mjs` (85 checks, ~4.5 min): 1997, 2002, 2003, 2005, 2009, 2014, 2025 with a real GameSim league (29/30 clubs, 1189/1230 games, all 82-game, W = L, points balance), real playoffs (first round best-of-5 in 1997 and 2002, best-of-7 after), one champion, honours once, reload changes nothing, rollover clean.
- **Design note:** the contract decision reads trust AFTER the playoffs now, because playoff games feed the existing trust rule. In the script the player's club is a fake 82-0 and trust is pinned at 80, so a weak player's trust falls and he is "cut" even after a title (1997). A real career reaches the playoffs with the trust the season gave him; still, say if you want the contract to read the end-of-regular-season trust instead.

### 2026-11-19 — Avatar Creator 2.0, P0 body-deformation artifacts — code-puppy-7319ef
- Seen on the real art (`scripts/qa/avatar-build-sheet.mjs`, new: the real figure at any build, side by side, with a hand/belt close-up): a wide waist threw the hands out into claws and broke the wrists. Cause: the waist fades in over the very rows of the hand (790-850), so the top of the hand moved less than the bottom (a shear). `rowOps` now keeps the arms and hands rigid against the waist (the shoulders still carry them); the torso and shorts are drawn first, the arms over them (as in the art). The arm segment reaches `ARM_INNER_PAD` = 4 px past its measured inner edge: the anchors were measured on alpha > 40, and the faint finger fringe left in the torso segment was carried off as thin skin-coloured streaks when the waist narrowed.
- `BUILD_RANGE`: waist 0.22 -> 0.14, arms 0.36 -> 0.30 (Broad's own arm size; covers the Slim / Broad waist). Sliders stay 0-100, saved values keep their meaning (a saved arms=100 now draws the new, smaller extreme). Presets (`body: broad / slim`) are untouched.
- Checks: shape tests 15 (new: arms/hands are exactly as without the waist on every row from shoulder to finger tip for waist -0.14..0.14; shoulders still carry the arms), avatar/jersey/my-world unit tests 192, `avatar-body-2d` 22/22 (waist now measured BELOW the hands, row 920: slim -12% / broad +10%, exactly the presets), `jersey-equip-2d` 46/46, `avatar-creator-2d` 104/104, `avatar-2d-default` 35/35, `avatar-portrait` (3D, presets, portrait, no-WebGL fallback) 82/82, full suite 3359/3359.
- Known, accepted (not tuned): the shoulder 100 "shelf" corner, the arm 100 outward bow, the S-shaped bend of the shorts side when the waist narrows (the fade ends below the hem), a ragged side trim on light shorts (also in the unmodified default, so it is in the source art).
- Not mine: `scripts/qa/avatar-creator-slice.mjs` fails on a clean main (reads the 3D canvas look as null); left alone.

### 2026-11-19 — Avatar Creator 2.0, P1 female art: BUILT, option still OFF until you approve it visually — code-puppy-83e68b
- **What exists now**: `assets/avatar2d/female/` = the full 10-layer Style A set (body, short hair front/back, brows, eyes, jersey top/bottom + trims, shoes), 704 KB, validator 13 pass / 0 warn / 0 fail, rebuild is byte-identical. Built by `scripts/avatar/art2d/build_female.py` from the approved master `src/female/master.jpg` (the dressed full-body female; 683x1024, upscaled 1.42x to the 1024x1536 canvas). Same method as the male build: every visible pixel comes from the master, so hair / jersey / shorts / socks / shoes / eyes / brows register to it by construction; what the clothes hide is inpainted; the bald reference `src/female/bald.jpg` supplies the scalp only (registered onto the master's head: NCC 0.931, scale 0.97, shift (0, 1) = the same face). The master's baked jersey number is erased (Poisson clone) because the game draws its own.
- **Why the four separately generated pieces were not used**: they do not register to the master. Measured: the uniform piece is 1.38x the master's scale, the hair piece 2.7-4.6x with a different cut; stacking them cannot work, so they stay in `src/female/*-REJECTED.jpg` for provenance only. If a better regenerated set arrives it can replace `master.jpg` / `bald.jpg` and the script rebuilds everything.
- **Engineering**: `js/ui/avatar2d/avatar2dAnchors.js` holds the measured body numbers per presentation (male tables moved verbatim from avatar2dShape.js; female measured on its own layers: shorts ~680-856 vs male 850-1000, legs/socks/hands rows, torso axis 512, number anchor, portrait crop). `avatar2dShape.js` functions take the set as an optional last argument (default = male, so every male call is unchanged; a parity test pins it); the renderer passes the drawn presentation's set to the shape, the jersey number and the portrait crop. `scripts/measure/avatar-anchor-tables.mjs` prints the tables from the PNGs, and `tests/avatarFemaleArt.test.js` (11 tests) checks the tables against the same pixels, so art and numbers cannot drift apart silently.
- **Verified on the real renderer** (a scratch copy where the female set stood in for the male one; the repo's male art untouched): default + 12 slider combinations incl. all-0 / all-100 / height 150 and 220 -> both hands whole, no broken wrists, silhouette continuous, feet not distorted (`/tmp/cls-qa/female/sheet-final.png`); the real My World flow with a purchased jersey worn -> purple shirt, teal shorts, number centred on the chest (`/tmp/cls-qa/female/jersey/desktop-wearing-neon.png`). `scripts/qa/jersey-equip-2d.mjs` gave 42/44 there; the 2 FAILs are the script sampling the shirt colour at a MALE pixel position (it lands on the female arm; the screenshot shows the purple shirt) - the script needs a presentation-aware sample point before the female option is switched on.
- **Gate**: the female option is still OFF (`ART.presentations` = male only, `PRESENTATIONS.female.art = false`; a saved `presentation: "female"` still draws the male art and reports the fallback). `tests/avatarFemaleArt.test.js` pins this on purpose. **To switch it on after visual approval**: add `"female"` to `ART.presentations` + `art: true`, update the pinning tests, then run the browser QA that is NOT done yet: creator/jersey/body scripts with presentation-aware sampling, age 60 and the Shaved scalp seam (V7), dark colourways (V8), 390px mobile, save/reload, an old save, the portrait. Check also that the Identity tab can actually pick the presentation.
- **Known art limits (not blockers, judge by eye)**: the source is 683x1024, so the face is soft (eyes ~26 px on the canvas); loose single hair wisps outside the hair mass are dropped; faint grey streaks at the inner ends of the shorts hem; arms=100 bows the forearm outward and height 220 leaves a tight top margin (same as the male, accepted in P0). Face variants (eyes / brows / nose / mouth sets, face shapes, a separate beard layer) are NOT done: they need the blank-face base first and are the next art step after the female body is playable.
- Checks: shape / jersey / renderer / layers / face unit tests 60 (unchanged), new female tests 11, full suite 3446 of 3447 before one test fix (the old "shipped art passes the validator" test now skips the folder of a presentation that is not switched on) -> 23/23 on the two validator test files after it; lint clean.

### 2026-11-19 — Career 2.0 decision 4: fragment redemptions (specialisation + legacy jersey) SHIPPED; audit of decisions 1-3 — code-puppy-00e1af
- **Audit of current main against the final decisions** (read-only, nothing reimplemented): 1 promises = done (`742c6ff`, `8ab8bdb`; Inbox, the Relationships dev-settle button and Reborn all go through `settleGamePromises`). One gap left, NOT touched: the coaching challenge keeps its own `tacticalLearning.recordPromise/settlePromises` (in-game "star touches / defense holds / comeback" promises, settled with `Date.now()`, no shared idempotency ledger). 2 rivalries = done (auto + story formed from real games in `recordRivalryMeetings`, player challenge via `challengeRival` + candidates UI, `recordRivalryGame` with the real gameId, takeover window). 3 epiphany = done (`settleEpiphany`: evidence families, controlled roll, ledger + retract = idempotent; note its default rng is `Math.random`, deterministic tests inject one). 4 fragments = only intensive training existed -> this entry.
- **What fragments buy now** (Reborn-only ledger, still earned only from real game evidence, nothing touches account gold or My Team cards):
  - intensive training (unchanged): −3, this week's training XP ×1.5.
  - **skill specialisation** (new): −2, once per week, needs this week's focus. For that week only, the training levels go into the attributes of a skill the player ALREADY has (its awakening family: shooter -> three, playmaker -> pass+iq, scorer -> mid+scoring, finisher -> paint+strength, rebounder -> rebound+strength, rim -> block+defense, thief -> steal+defense). It steers growth, adds none: the season level cap and the real-career ceilings are untouched (test: same level count as the plain focus, a capped attribute takes nothing). Only skills in an awakening family qualify (awakened skills always do).
  - **legacy jersey** (new): −10, one per season, needs the identity revealed (the name is printed on it). The player's own season jersey as a `story` template (never sold, `number: null`, team colours only), into the run's jersey collection -> mirrored to the account by the existing `mirrorJerseysToAccount`, labelled 传承 / Legacy in My World with its story, wearable on the 2D figure.
- **Bug fixed on the way**: the rollover deleted `player.training` but kept `fragments.intensiveWeek`, so next season's same week number counted as already bought: the button was blocked AND the week got ×1.5 for free. `seasonResetFragments` now clears the weekly fields at rollover (balance and history carry). Mutation-checked.
- Files: `js/reborn/fragments.js` (rules), `js/reborn/training.js` (`trainingPlan(reborn, week)`), `js/career/playerGrowth.js` (`trainedAttributes` honours `plan.attributes`; other callers unchanged), `js/reborn/season.js` (passes the game week, report `specialty`), `js/reborn/contract.js` (rollover reset), `js/collectibles/jerseyCatalog.js` (`storyTemplate` shared by `exchangeTemplate` + new `legacyTemplate`), `js/collectibles/jerseyCollection.js` + `js/ui/collectibles/jerseyCollectionView.js` (legacy source), `js/ui/reborn/fragmentsView.js` + `rebornView.js` (panel, clicks; `playerCardFor` replaces two copies of the card recipe).
- Checks: `tests/rebornFragmentSpend.test.js` 7 new + `tests/rebornFragments.test.js` 6 = 13/13; full suite 3461/3461; lint clean (one old warning in rivalry.js); browser `scripts/qa/reborn-fragments-redeem.mjs` all PASS desktop + 390px (seed 14 -> specialise −2 -> legacy −10 -> reload keeps both -> real game report names the specialisation -> My World shows the 传承 jersey -> worn on the 2D figure; no page errors, no overflow). Screens: `/tmp/cls-qa/reborn-fragments-redeem/`.
- **Muse QA handoff**: earn-then-spend over a real multi-week run (no seeding); specialisation across a week boundary (must stop next week); season rollover with an intensive/specialisation bought in the last week; unrevealed random-draw identity (legacy blocked until reveal); guest (no account: jersey stays in the run); English mode; old Reborn saves without the new fields; two seasons = two legacy jerseys.

### 2026-11-19 - P0 test stability: `onlineHardening` flake (code-puppy-cafd6e)
- **Could not reproduce, so no root cause is claimed (BACKLOG B75).** Signature: `read ECONNRESET` after 293 ms (shorter than every pass at 362-1263 ms, so the first handshake), stack only `TCP.onStreamRead`. Refuted by experiment: scrypt + 3 s timeout under load (the failure is not a timeout; 20 runs under 24-30 busy processes and 12 runs of all `online*` files concurrently were clean), wildcard-vs-127.0.0.1 port collision (1500 server lifetimes, zero overlapping ports), an uncaught error in the close race (ws installs a NOOP socket error handler), first connect from a fresh process (800 new processes, wildcard and loopback: 0 failures). 10 full-suite runs after the failures, 6 of them as two concurrent suites: all green.
- Change (test only): `withServer` annotates ECONN* errors with server address / uptime / pid / platform and rethrows. Assertions untouched, no retry, no longer timeouts. Verified: a forced ECONNRESET still fails the test and prints the context. If it fires again, B75 says what to read and what to change (bind the test servers to 127.0.0.1 if the address is the `::` wildcard).

### 2026-11-20 — Live match: Broadcast-first layout, Slice 1 (desktop + the mobile order) (code-puppy-d8e9bb)
- Court 72% / feed 28% (own column, full row height), court 16:9 (4:3 on phones, as tall as before), coach panel collapsed to ONE action bar under the court (叫暂停 / 大喊 / 速度 / 暂停模拟 / 教练席▾) and opened by itself in every dead-ball window (timeout, quarter break, injury, event); the court shrinks but never disappears. No id changed; `js/ui/coachDrawer.js` (pure state + thin wiring), `css/matchBroadcast.css`, `syncPauseUI` gains one line. GameSim untouched.
- Court size, same script both builds (`scripts/qa/match-broadcast-layout.mjs <url> <out> [--baseline]`): 1440x900 574x300 -> 979x520 (13% -> 39% of the viewport); 1280x800 508x300 -> 866x420; Gold 3D canvas 568x294 -> 973x514. Phone coach panel was 5128px tall; now the court + the whole action bar fit the first screen.
- Real regression found and fixed on the way: `matchEventCards.js` anchored its toast on `#play-feed` inside the court column; with the feed moved it threw `insertBefore`. The anchor is now used only if it is really a child.
- Verified: layout QA ~20 checks x 3 viewports ALL PASS; match-3d-gold all passed; gold-tactics-board 30/30; court-panel-height (updated: its "left column = right panel height" assertion is obsolete, the no-drift invariant is kept and now covers feed + row) ALL PASS; unit 3576/3576.
- NOT done yet (next slices): Slice 2 de-duplicate the two tempo/focus control sets (takeover panel vs dead-ball panel) and add the Lineup / Tactics / Defense / Rotation tabs; Slice 3 phone bottom sheet (today the opened panel just scrolls the page) and optionally hide the global top bar on the match screen; Slice 4 camera (the 3D players are still clustered at the far basket with ~40% empty background above the court; a bigger box did not fix that).
- Pre-existing, not mine: `scripts/qa/match-events.mjs` is 15 pass / 3 fail on a clean origin/main too: it treats the new Report-Only CSP console messages as page errors (Muse S-P2-5).

### 2026-11-20 — DB Phase 0 audit (code-puppy-d8e9bb)
- `docs/implementation/DB-PHASE0-AUDIT.md`. Read-only; no code changed. Key facts: a backend already exists but is duel-only (JSON files in the home dir); local accounts have **no authentication**; gold/rewards are computed client-side, so a cloud wallet is "synced, not trusted"; the account store has no schema version.
- Proposed first slice: cloud save of the long-career root (`cls_root_v3`) behind a flag, compare-and-swap on `revision`, injectable transport so it is fully testable offline. Needs 4 decisions from Zoro (listed at the end of the doc), notably who creates the Supabase project.

### 2026-11-20 — TASK FOR MUSE: Career 2.0 end-to-end QA (decision by Zoro; B75 closed as "Observability Added / Root Cause Unknown")
**Owner: Muse.** Real-browser E2E of Career 2.0 on current `origin/main`. Do not expand Career 2.0 with new mechanics during this QA; fix only confirmed P0/P1 regressions, each in an isolated commit. Use focused tests + browser smoke; no repeated full-suite runs.
Scope: (1) unified Promise Engine (create / fulfil / break / cross-season settle, real match evidence, idempotent); (2) Rivalry Auto / Story / Challenge (all three origins, no duplicate rivalry `gameId`, challenge limits + duplicate check); (3) evidence-driven Epiphany (no trigger without evidence, no random awakening, no duplicate reward); (4) Skill Fragments (earn, spend, intensive training, specialisation, legacy honours; cannot be spent twice); (5) multi-season 1997 -> 1998 -> 1999 (year, roster, schedule, relationships; rollover keeps relationships); (6) save/reload mid-game, end of season, playoffs (no double settlement) + guest vs registered account isolation (honours, collection, fragments never cross).
For every feature: real UI entry -> act -> check persisted state -> check the game evidence -> reload -> confirm consistency -> try to settle twice. Say which events were triggered naturally and which were injected fixtures. Deterministic seeds where possible.
Deliverable: a feature-by-feature PASS / FAIL / NOT TESTED matrix; every FAIL with repro steps, seed + mode, expected vs actual, state evidence, screenshot, severity.
Measure while you are in there (do not build anything for them): **discoverability** (does a player find promises / rivalries / epiphany / fragments?), **trigger frequency** (key events a player actually meets in one normal season), **feedback quality** (can a player tell why a reward came, a relationship moved, a promise failed?).
Pointers from code-puppy (saves you time, none of it is a verdict): existing browser scripts in `scripts/qa/`: `reborn-multiseason.mjs`, `reborn-playoffs.mjs`, `reborn-playoffs-seasons.mjs`, `reborn-fragments*.mjs` (buy / redeem / season), `career-epiphany.mjs`, `career-relationships.mjs`, `promise-settle.mjs`, `reborn-entry.mjs`, `reborn-rivalry.mjs`. `reborn-rivalry.mjs` probes `.reborn-takeover-overlay`, but the real takeover UI is a Shell modal, so that probe only passes when no dialog appears. `match-events.mjs` currently fails 3 console-error checks on a clean main because of the Report-Only CSP messages (not a Career bug). The playoffs "missed"/"unavailable" paths and the Finals-loss honour were unit-tested but have had no long browser run.
B75 (`ECONNRESET` in onlineHardening) stays open and non-blocking; `withServer` now also logs the dialed URL and the number of sockets the server had accepted. If it recurs, record those plus the listening address and process exit status. Do not change the production bind address because of an IPv6 suspicion: controlled A/B in a test environment first.

### 2026-11-20 — P0 match presentation sync: audit + S1a (identity and ordering) (code-puppy-d8e9bb)
- Audit and numbers: `docs/implementation/PRESENTATION-SYNC-AUDIT.md`; reproduce with `node scripts/measure/presentation-sync.mjs` (real GameSim + real match3dController on a fake clock, shared rig `tests/helpers/presentationHarness.js`).
- Root cause of "Harden shoots but the screen shows someone else": the derived POSSESSION_START (fired after a possession, snapshot after it, same clock) was sorted by clock in front of the possession it had just closed, so 3D drew the NEXT team on offense while the shot played. Shooter on offense in the 3D court: 1/30, 0/10, 0/5 (1x/2x/3x) -> 33/33, 11/11, 6/6.
- S1a changes: `GameSim.emit` stamps a monotonic `seq` (continues across save/reload; no RNG; nothing in quiet games); the live normalizer orders by arrival and gives every event an `eventId`; the controller drains before adding a possession start; beats and `onShot` carry `eventId`. All 20 golden games hash identically to the old fixture once `seq` is stripped (results and RNG unchanged); `tests/fixtures/simGolden.json` re-recorded only because the payload gained a field.
- Still open (S2/S3/S4): the 3D ball still follows the PG slot, not the real ball handler; the feed/scoreboard still paint immediately, so at 2x/3x ~3 of 4 shots are never shown in 3D and the commentary is several possessions ahead.
- Verified: new `tests/presentationSync.test.js` 7/7 (mutation-checked: undoing either fix fails it), 43 existing presentation tests, full suite, `scripts/qa/match-3d-gold.mjs` all passed.

### 2026-11-20 — Presentation sync S2: the 3D ball follows the real holder (code-puppy-d8e9bb)
- New `js/match/presentation/ballOwner.js` (pure: who has the ball after each event, from GameSim ids only), holder transitions on choreographed beats, `setBallOwner` / `onPass` on `court3d.js`, `onSkip` in the scheduler so compressed beats still apply their outcome, `coach` lines with `meta.pass` normalised as passes. GameSim, its outcomes and RNG are untouched.
- Numbers (real GameSim + real controller, `node scripts/measure/presentation-sync.mjs 60 sync-1`): ball with the shooter when the shot begins 30/32 at 1x (was 14/33); passes start at the real holder 13/14; possessions open with an offensive holder 38/38. The leftovers are GameSim gaps (no pass event after an offensive rebound), documented in `docs/implementation/PRESENTATION-SYNC-AUDIT.md`.
- Tests: `tests/ballOwner.test.js`, the new S2 blocks in `presentationSync`, `matchPresentationView`, `matchPresentationScheduler`, `matchPresentationNormalizer`, `court3dReplay` (mutation-checked: dropping `onSkip` or the repass rule fails them). Browser: `scripts/qa/match-3d-sync.mjs` (desktop + mobile trace, screenshots in /tmp/cls-qa/s2-sync).
- Still open: S3 (one cursor for feed/scoreboard/3D, bounded backlog at 2x/3x) and S4 (labels/camera: the screenshot shows ten overlapping, truncated name plates and a tiny ball).

### 2026-11-20 — "James Harden at C": one seating rule for every starting five (code-puppy-d8e9bb)
- Root cause: NOT the roster data (Harden is SG, Cheeks PG; I audited all 15,066 player-season cards with `scripts/measure/position-audit.mjs`: no C under 199 cm, no PF under 188 cm). Three code paths seated a five greedily ("best player picks first, the last man takes the hole that is left"): `careerEconomy.seatByPosition` (Gold CPU/auto fives), `app.js autoAssignUniquePositions` (setup auto-fill) and `gameplan.resolveStarterPositionCollisions` (stacked starters). `reseatLineup` (substitutions) was optimal but with a linear cost that happily wrecks one player to keep the rest perfect.
- Fix: `js/engine/lineupSlots.js` `seatPlayers` tries every arrangement and keeps the cheapest; all four paths use it. Cost = position misfit (engine's own `positionFitScore`), SQUARED so one disaster costs more than several small shifts, plus an inside/outside term (`insideRating`: height, strength, boards, paint, rim; `outsideRating`: passing, shooting, speed) so when there is truly no big the best inside player is the one who plays C. A player tall enough (>=201 cm PF, >=206 cm C) is not "unfit": the engine's fit score gates on strength, which sits at 35-58 on nearly every card (Giannis 41), so a 7-footer scored the same 38 as a 6'3" guard. The engine's fit score itself is unchanged.
- `node scripts/measure/lineup-seating.mjs 4000` (real cards, mixed eras): a player under 200 cm at C 180 -> 55 (all in fives with nobody tall), at C while a 206 cm+ teammate is on the floor 106 -> 1; "disasters" (fit < 45) 2172 -> 866.
- Side effect, on purpose: GameSim's substitution re-seat uses the same cost, so seeded games drift. Paired 60 games x 6 matchups: pts 102.79 -> 102.63, every other team stat within 0.1. Re-recorded `tests/fixtures/simGolden.json`; re-picked fixed seeds in `rebornTitleExchange` (tx-123 -> tx-156), `rebornFranchises` (franchise-check-2); `rebornFragmentQa` no longer pins an exact standout count (asserts what it is about: enough to buy, and the ledger balance conserves).
- Open for a human call: Giannis 2015 (SG) and 2016 (PG) are 211 cm cards with a guard label (BRef lists him as a forward); I did not change data.
- Tests: `tests/lineupSeating.test.js` (10; real cards incl. the Harden five; mutation-checked: linear cost, no height rule, no inside/outside term, CPU five back on the greedy loop all fail). Browser: `scripts/qa/gold-tactics-board.mjs` all passed.

### 2026-11-20 — Play-feed "oval with the player's name" (code-puppy-d8e9bb)
- Cause (reproduced in a real browser, `scripts/qa/feed-avatar-broken.mjs`): a headshot <img> that fails to load (e.g. a hotlinked photo of an obscure 1970s player) is rendered by Chrome as an INLINE box that ignores width/height, so `.feed-avatar`'s 18x18 became 112x18 and, with `.headshot`'s round border and dark background, an oval printing the alt text (the player's name).
- Fix: `.headshot` is now `display:inline-block; overflow:hidden; font-size:0; color:transparent` (css/style.css), so a broken photo stays the same small round chip everywhere (feed, cards, rosters). Loaded photos render identically. The retry-then-generated-avatar fallback (`imgErrorFallback.js`) is unchanged.
- Verified: broken headshot 112x18 -> 18x18 on desktop and 390px; Gold browser smoke and photo/feed/fallback unit tests (59) pass.

### 2026-11-20 — 3D Arena system: A1 (venues) + A2 (stands, crowd, courtside) (code-puppy-d8e9bb)
- Phase 0: camera is high on the +x side looking toward -x; `court3d.js` was already 988 lines, so all venue code lives in `js/engine/arena/` (presets, floor texture, hoop trim, lights, pure layout, instancing helpers, crowd, courtside furniture, screens, environment) and `court3d.js` only gained wiring. Pure presentation: it reads the match snapshot (scoreboard) and the clock (crowd idle bob) and nothing else; GameSim, scores and RNG are untouched. The venue is built defensively: no InstancedMesh (test fake) = no stands; a floor-paint or environment failure falls back to flat wood / no stands, never a broken match view.
- A1: three original arenas as data (`arenaPresets.js`): Classic Hardwood (parquet, green key), Modern Pro (long plank, navy key, cyan LED), Retro 90s (herringbone, red key, amber LED). Procedural, seeded floors; painted keys and centre circle; eight-point star + the game's own name (no real logos / court designs); shooter's square, backboard frame, padded stanchion; warm / broadcast light rigs; Low / Medium / High quality profiles (`auto` = Low on a small or touch screen); `?arena=` / `?arenaQuality=` and a device-local preference; a venue picker next to the 2D/3D toggle.
- A2: far and end stands with a walkway and railings, a low near stand kept below the camera sightline, ~2,500 seats (High) as ONE instanced step mesh + two instanced people meshes, home/away benches with players and a standing head coach, scorer's table with officials, sponsor LED boards (fictional brands), shot clocks above the stanchions, light trusses, walls, and a hung scoreboard that shows the real score / clock / period from the snapshot (redrawn only when it changes). The ~60 court line meshes are now one instanced mesh.
- Measured in a real browser (`node scripts/qa/arena-screens.mjs`, swiftshader): draw calls 149 before -> 100 desktop / 98 mobile now; triangles ~125K desktop (High), ~46K mobile (Low). Targets from the brief: desktop <= 250 calls / 300K, mobile <= 120 / 100K. Frame rate is NOT measured (swiftshader is not a GPU): real-phone FPS is still open and is Muse's.
- Tests: `tests/arenaA1.test.js` (10) and `tests/arenaA2.test.js` (12, real THREE r187 in Node): no seat on the floor, near stand under the sightline, draw-call and triangle budgets per quality, crowd deterministic and Low is a subset of High, scoreboard only from the snapshot and redrawn only on change, dispose empties the scene. Mutation-checked (floor tint, hoop trim direction, stand start inside the court, stand starting too high, walkway rise).
- Known cosmetics / not done: text on the left LED board reads mirrored; player name plates still pile up (S4); crowd is static except a slow head bob (reactions, referees, cheerleaders, sounds = A3, must consume the presentation timeline); street / beach / international scenes + the quality picker UI = A4. [2026-11-20] Lychee：认领用户报的死锁 bug——每日抽卡与我的球员页面返回键互指。已复现（真实 navPush/navPop）：首页进抽卡后，抽卡返回用 show("collection") 前进式跳转，把 draw 压进栈，球员页返回 goBack() 又弹回抽卡，draw ⇄ collection 永远到不了首页；从球员页进抽卡则因 truncate 规则偶然不触发。修法：抽卡返回改为 pop 语义（navBack.js 加 resolveDrawBack：栈顶是 collection 就消费掉，否则不动；app.js 里 navPopping 下 show("collection") 不压栈，仍调 openCollection() 重渲染），不改球员页返回与静态目标表。验证：tests/backButtons.test.js 补回归（两种进入路径都走回首页 + 旧行为循环钉住）+ 聚焦 + 全套重跑。不碰 sim.js、roster.json、存档版本、modeCatalog、M4-A 表单与 profile.js 字段、职业创建入口、Code 的 Slice 1 / GameSim 校准认领范围、B76（Code）、B61（Code）、Muse 的 Career 2.0 E2E QA。

[2026-11-20] Lychee：用户报的抽卡/球员页返回死锁已修（9404151）。根因：抽卡返回是前进式 show("collection")，从首页进抽卡后把 draw 压进栈，球员页返回 goBack() 又弹回抽卡，两页互跳、永远到不了首页（真实 navPush/navPop 复现）。修法：navBack.js 加 resolveDrawBack（栈顶是 collection 就消费掉，否则不动），app.js 抽卡返回在 navPopping 下 show("collection") 不压栈，仍调 openCollection() 重渲染；球员页返回不动。验证：backButtons 23/23（新增 6 回归：两种进入路径都走回首页、旧行为循环钉住、接线钉住；变异检查：resolveDrawBack 失效时恰好 2 个测试失败），全套 3694/3694，lint 干净。另：24 小时定时刷新已于 17:36 按 0b 规则停用（job 已禁用，无下一轮）。
